| 1093 | `PMC11408469_01` | `PMC11408469_01::COVID-19` | `E1` | `CURRENT_ACTIVE` | `ACTIVE_COMORBIDITY` | **Quote** (`2379..2432`): "2 C, and the COVID-19 nucleic acid test was positive." — This single-patient case report describes an acute myeloid leukemia (NUP98::RARG) patient presenting with a fever of 39.2 C and a positive COVID-19 nucleic acid test on admission who was treated with Paxlovid and achieved viral clearance one week later. Definitive E1 molecular confirmation of acute COVID-19 on admission satisfies all VPD criteria. |
| 1114 | `PMC11756012_01` | `PMC11756012_01::COVID-19` | `E1` | `CURRENT_ACTIVE` | `ACTIVE_COMORBIDITY` | **Quote** (`2560..2684`): "Abdominal CT and real-time polymerase chain reaction confirmed the presence of a liver abscess and coronavirus disease 2019." — This single-patient case report describes a pancreatic cancer patient who developed fever and abdominal pain following modified FOLFIRINOX chemotherapy in December 2022, with real-time PCR confirming coronavirus disease 2019 alongside a Candida albicans liver abscess, successfully treated with a 5-week course of azvudine and fluconazole. Definitive E1 RT-PCR confirmation and antiviral treatment satisfy all VPD criteria. |
| 1119 | `PMC11773861_01` | `PMC11773861_01::COVID-19` | `E2` | `CURRENT_ACTIVE` | `ACTIVE_COMORBIDITY` | **Quote** (`262..342`): "A novel coronavirus antigen test was performed, and it showed positive findings." — This single-patient case report describes an 83-year-old man hospitalized with generalized erythema who tested positive on a novel coronavirus antigen test on day 1 and received remdesivir before progressing to Stevens-Johnson syndrome and toxic epidermal necrolysis. Virologically confirmed active SARS-CoV-2 infection via positive antigen test (E2) and remdesivir therapy satisfies all VPD criteria. |
| 1141 | `PMC11705338_01` | `PMC11705338_01::COVID-19` | `E1` | `CURRENT_ACTIVE` | `ACTIVE_COMORBIDITY` | **Quote** (`6085..6181`): "COVID-19 infection was diagnosed based on positive antigenic and molecular tests for SARS-CoV-2." — This single-patient case report describes a child with a TERC variant telomere biology disorder who tested negative for SARS-CoV-2 on his first admission, but during his third hospitalization in November 2022 for fever, profound neutropenia, and HLH was diagnosed with COVID-19 based on positive antigenic and molecular tests for SARS-CoV-2. Definitive E1 molecular and antigen confirmation during the active hospitalization satisfies all VPD criteria. |
| 1151 | `PMC11810938_01` | `PMC11810938_01::COVID-19` | `E1` | `CURRENT_ACTIVE` | `ACTIVE_COMORBIDITY` | **Quote** (`3806..3967`): "Four months post-transplant, the patient presented with mild upper respiratory infection due to SARS-CoV-2 resulting in positive PCR through nasopharyngeal swab." — This single-patient case report describes a patient with DOCK8 deficiency who presented four months post-allogeneic hematopoietic stem cell transplantation with an upper respiratory infection and a positive nasopharyngeal swab PCR for SARS-CoV-2, achieving full recovery. Definitive E1 molecular confirmation of intercurrent SARS-CoV-2 infection during post-transplant follow-up satisfies all VPD criteria. |
| 1159 | `PMC11654242_02` | `PMC11654242_02::COVID-19` | `E2` | `CURRENT_ACTIVE` | `ACTIVE_COMORBIDITY` | **Quote** (`189..283`): "Patient 2 tested positive for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and SARS coronavirus 2 withal." — This single-patient case vignette (Patient 2) describes a 23-day-old male newborn hospitalized with fever, nasal obstruction, and acute bronchiolitis who tested positive for both respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and SARS coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2). Virologically confirmed active SARS-CoV-2 co-infection during the acute bronchiolitis hospitalization satisfies E2 and all VPD criteria. |
| 1195 | `PMC12176811_02` | `PMC12176811_02::COVID-19` | `E1` | `RECENT_EPISODIC` | `ACTIVE_COMORBIDITY` | **Quote** (`172..216`): "Respiratory SARS-CoV-2 RNA PCR was positive." — This single-patient case report describes a male child with RANBP2-associated acute necrotizing encephalopathy (ANE) who presented at 24 months of age with febrile encephalopathy, seizures, and bilateral thalamic MRI lesions with a positive respiratory SARS-CoV-2 RNA PCR test, treated with IVIG and dexamethasone, followed by a recurrent episode at 30 months. Definitive E1 PCR confirmation of SARS-CoV-2-triggered ANE in the reported clinical course satisfies all VPD criteria. |
| 1201 | `PMC12253983_02` | `PMC12253983_02::COVID-19` | `E2` | `CURRENT_ACTIVE` | `ACTIVE_COMORBIDITY` | **Quote** (`225..284`): "In addition, the patient was found to be COVID-19 positive." — This single-patient case report describes a young male with AIDS (CD4 count 28) and severe concurrent mpox and HSV-1 proctitis who was also found to be COVID-19 positive upon hospital readmission with dehydration and prerenal AKI. Confirmed active COVID-19 co-infection during the hospitalization satisfies E2 and all VPD criteria. |
| 1219 | `PMC12158593_04` | `PMC12158593_04::COVID-19` | `E2` | `RECENT_EPISODIC` | `ACTIVE_COMORBIDITY` | **Quote** (`51..244`): "After an infection with severe acute respiratory syndrome caused by Coronavirus 2, his condition deteriorated rapidly, resulting in a persistent confusional state with significant fluctuations." — This single-patient case vignette describes a 73-year-old patient with mixed dementia whose condition deteriorated rapidly after an infection with severe acute respiratory syndrome caused by Coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2), resulting in a persistent confusional state, agitation, and hospital admission. Clinician-confirmed recent SARS-CoV-2 infection directly precipitating the inpatient admission satisfies E2 and VPD criteria. |
| 1237 | `PMC12258869_01` | `PMC12258869_01::COVID-19` | `E2` | `CURRENT_ACTIVE` | `ACTIVE_COMORBIDITY` | **Quote** (`2887..3024`): "Her treatment was complicated, with anemia requiring blood transfusion, COVID-19 infection, and weight loss requiring PEG tube placement." — This single-patient case report describes a 48-year-old woman undergoing concurrent cisplatin/etoposide chemoradiation for sinonasal teratocarcinosarcoma whose active treatment course was complicated by a COVID-19 infection, anemia requiring transfusion, and weight loss requiring PEG tube placement. Clinician-confirmed intercurrent COVID-19 during active therapy satisfies E2 and VPD criteria. |
| 1240 | `PMC12478278_01` | `PMC12478278_01::COVID-19` | `E2` | `CURRENT_ACTIVE` | `ACTIVE_COMORBIDITY` | **Quote** (`3049..3143`): "The patient tested positive for SARS-CoV2, with no additional detection of malaria and dengue." — This single-patient case report describes a 45-year-old woman admitted with 14 days of fever and tachypnea who had a recent COVID-19 infection within the past 14 days, tested positive for SARS-CoV-2 during hospitalization, and carried a principal diagnosis of septic shock secondary to acute cholangitis with SARS-CoV-2 infection. Virologically confirmed active SARS-CoV-2 co-infection satisfies E2 and all VPD criteria. |
| 1248 | `PMC12644420_01` | `PMC12644420_01::COVID-19` | `E2` | `CURRENT_ACTIVE` | `ACTIVE_COMORBIDITY` | **Quote** (`5668..5768`): "5 years, the patient died of COVID-19-triggered severe respiratory failure, seen in Figure 2(m)-(p)." — This single-patient case report describes a 38-year-old woman with HPS4-mutated Hermansky-Pudlak syndrome and pulmonary fibrosis stabilized on nintedanib for 1.5 years who subsequently died of COVID-19-triggered severe respiratory failure, documented radiologically on chest HRCT (Figure 2m-p). Clinician- and radiologically-confirmed fatal COVID-19 respiratory failure satisfies E2 and all VPD criteria. |
